- **justified-consumption**: 当用户在撰写文案时需要消除读者"花钱的负罪感"，或产品本身属于"享受型/非刚需"品类时调用此 skill。核心任务是将购买理由重新归类到四大正当消费类别之一（上进、送礼、健康、孩子），让读者觉得"这笔钱花得对"。不适用于功能性刚需产品（如冰箱、洗衣机），也不适用于 B2B 场景。关键触发信号：用户提到"客单价高""客户犹豫""觉得贵""不好意思买""享受型产品"等。 ([kangarooking/viral-copywriting-skill](https://github.com/kangarooking/viral-copywriting-skill/tree/HEAD/justified-consumption) · ★94)
- **justify**: Audits changes for additive bias and Iron Law compliance. Use when reviewing completed work before merging or after AI-assisted implementation. ([athola/claude-night-market](https://github.com/athola/claude-night-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/imbue/skills/justify) · ★350)
- **justify**: Judges, on evidence, whether one named artifact (a file, doc section, dependency, component) had and still has a reason to exist, or whether a proposal (a plan step, design option, or mechanism to add) is needed at all. Read-only. Use when the user asks whether a thing needs to exist or still earns its keep, or of a proposal whether it is overengineered, whether we need X, or whether there is a simpler way. Not for the enforcement surface (overengineering:audit). ([melodic-software/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/melodic-software/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/overengineering/skills/justify) · ★32)
- **justify**: Demand a sufficient reason for each element (code, plan step, or action) and recommend cutting whatever cannot earn its place. Use on /justify: defaults to the working-tree diff, "justify actions" audits the recent action transcript, or pass a file path or plan to audit that. Also triggers on: "does this need to be here", "is this justified", "what can we cut", "does this carry dead weight", "audit this diff", "is this code/step/action necessary". - **jwe-data-sources**: Use when choosing or defending the data behind a 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) manuscript — customs micro data (firm-product-destination), cross-country panels, and GVC / input-output decomposition (KWW value-added, ADB / WIOD MRIO). Steers away from generic provincial panels toward internationally-credible sources. - **jwe-fit-positioning**: Use to judge whether a manuscript's international / open-economy dimension genuinely holds up for 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy), or whether it is really a domestic paper that should be re-routed to 金融研究 / 中国工业经济 / 经济研究. Run this first, before drafting. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-World-Economy-Skills/skills/jwe-fit-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jwe-heterogeneity**: Use when a 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) manuscript's heterogeneity is thin (e.g. only east/central/west) — slicing instead by internationally-meaningful margins: exposure intensity, firm trade type, destination / partner country, and trade margins. Use after the main effect and channel are established. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-World-Economy-Skills/skills/jwe-heterogeneity) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jwe-identification**: Use when the identification strategy is the bottleneck for a 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) manuscript — external shocks (tariffs, trade agreements, exchange-rate reforms, FDI negative-list) as quasi-experiments, plus shift-share / Bartik and gravity designs. Stress-tests exogeneity and inference, including the modern shift-share critiques. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-World-Economy-Skills/skills/jwe-identification) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jwe-literature-review**: Use when a 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) literature review cites only domestic empirics and fails to enter the international trade / international finance lineage. Ensures dialogue with the field's classics and frontier (gravity, heterogeneous firms, GVC, open-economy macro, capital flows), not a citation pile. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-World-Economy-Skills/skills/jwe-literature-review) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jwe-policy-implication**: Use when writing the policy-implication section of a 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) manuscript — pitching at the opening-up policy level (trade, exchange rate, capital account, industrial opening) rather than as a domestic operational to-do list. Use after results, channels, and heterogeneity are settled. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-World-Economy-Skills/skills/jwe-policy-implication) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jwe-rebuttal**: Use when responding to 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) referee reports (R&R) — structuring a point-by-point response letter that addresses the journal's characteristic challenges (open-economy fit, shift-share / gravity inference, transmission channel, GVC measurement). Use after the manuscript has actually been revised. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-World-Economy-Skills/skills/jwe-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jwe-submission**: Use for the pre-submission preflight to 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) — checking footnote (页下注) conventions, reference list at the end, 内容提要 ≤ 300 chars + 3–5 keywords, bilingual title, blind-review anonymity, and the official online submission site (www.jweonline.cn). Use right before submitting; verify every rule against the journal's current 投稿须知. - **jwe-tables-figures**: Use when finalizing the main tables and figures of a 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) manuscript — trimming over-wide regression tables, standardizing notes / clustering / significance, and presenting open-economy-specific objects (event-study plots, gravity tables, GVC decompositions). Use after results are settled. - **jwe-topic-international**: Use to sharpen a 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) topic into a causal question seen through an international / open-economy lens — turning a vague "global" angle into a well-posed open-economy treatment-effect or general-equilibrium question. - **jwe-transmission-channel**: Use when a 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy) manuscript stops at "it promoted exports" without showing how an external shock propagates — making the trade / investment / financial / expectation channel explicit and testable, not a hand-wavy mechanism paragraph. - **jwe-workflow**: Use when deciding which jwe-* sub-skill to invoke next, or when sequencing manuscript work from open-economy positioning through rebuttal for 《世界经济》 (The Journal of World Economy).
